Can you fetch me a glass of water, please?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To retrieve; to bear towards; to go and get."],"tags":["ditransitive","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"If you put some new tyres on it, and clean it up a bit, the car should fetch about $5,000","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To obtain as price or equivalent; to sell for."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to fetch headway or sternway","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To bring or get within reach by going; to reach; to arrive at; to attain; to reach by sailing."],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["To bring oneself; to make headway; to veer; as, to fetch about; to fetch to windward."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To take (a breath); to heave (a sigh)."],"tags":["literary","rare","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To cause to come; to bring to a particular state."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to fetch a man to","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To recall from a swoon; to revive; sometimes with to."],"tags":["obsolete","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To reduce; to throw."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to fetch a compass;  to fetch a leap","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To accomplish; to achieve; to perform, with certain objects or actions."],"tags":["archaic","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To make (a pump) draw water by pouring water into the top and working the handle."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"fĕch","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/fɛt͡ʃ/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"enpr":"fŏch"},{"ipa":"/fɑt͡ʃ/"}],"synonyms":[{"word":"collect"}],"upstream_line":342206,"upstream_offset":1660429258,"upstream_record_sha256":"d85c0989d5b0898edd93dcfb8b44790544450b7a30347530d43e44a1457506f6","word":"fetch"}
